          await updateUser(userId, { balance: user.balance + winAmount });
          await logBotAction({ type: "game", peerId, userId, text: `[id${userId}|${fullName}] Ğ²Ñ‹Ğ¸Ğ³Ñ€Ğ°Ğ»(-Ğ°) ${winAmount.toLocaleString()}$ Ğ² Ñ€ÑƒĞ»ĞµÑ‚ĞºĞµ (Ğ¡Ñ‚Ğ°Ğ²ĞºĞ°: ${stake.toLocaleString()}$)` });
          return await sendResponse(`ğŸ° [id${userId}|${fullName}], Ğ²Ñ‹ Ğ²Ñ‹Ğ¸Ğ³Ñ€Ğ°Ğ»Ğ¸ ${winAmount.toLocaleString()}$ Ğ² Ñ€ÑƒĞ»ĞµÑ‚ĞºĞµ!`);

          await updateUser(userId, { balance: user.balance - stake });

        const pluralize = (num: number, forms: string[]) => {
          const MathAbs = Math.abs(num);
          const n = MathAbs % 100;
          const n1 = n % 10;
          if (n > 10 && n < 20) return forms[2];
          if (n1 > 1 && n1 < 5) return forms[1];
          if (n1 === 1) return forms[0];
          return forms[2];
